  • 1
    cat <<'EOF' > /home/c/Documents/code/ITIR-suite/SensibLaw/docs/ADR_LAYER3_SPAN_CONTRACT.md # ADR: Layer 3 Span-Only Role Hypotheses Date: 20...
    cat <<'EOF' > /home/c/Documents/code/ITIR-suite/SensibLaw/docs/ADR_LAYER3_SPAN_CONTRACT.md # ADR: Layer 3 Span-Only Role Hypotheses Date: 2026-02-05 Status: Proposed ## Context Layer 3 is where interpretive artifacts begin to appear (e.g., role-like noun candidates, subject/object hints, or procedural actors). These artifacts are useful, but they must not contaminate Layer 0/1/2 truth or imply ontology membership. Past drafts used entity-like names (e.g., "candidate legal entity"), which risked smuggling in legal commitments. ## Decision Layer 3 will store **span-only role hypotheses** and nothing stronger. A Layer 3 record: - MUST reference a stable text span: `(doc_id, rev_id, token_start, token_end)` or equivalent. - MAY include `role_hypothesis`, `extractor`, `evidence`, and `confidence` metadata. - MUST NOT assert cross-document identity or merge spans into a single global entity. - MUST be regenerable from Layer 0/2 outputs without reading or rewriting canonical text. Promotion into ontology tables is a separate, explicit step governed by auditable rules (e.g., defined-term detection, repeated independent spans, or modal-verb participation). Promotion never mutates Layer 3 artifacts; it references them. ## Consequences - Layer 3 artifacts are **pre-ontological** and **local to a span**. - Determinism is preserved: deleting Layer 3 tables and rebuilding must yield identical outputs given the same Layer 0/2 inputs. - Schema or storage for Layer 3 must remain isolated from ontology tables until promotion criteria are satisfied. ## Non-goals - Defining the promotion rule set (documented separately). - Building extraction pipelines or heuristics in this ADR. - Changing Layer 0/1/2 storage semantics. ## Follow-ups - Define a CandidateRoleSpan (or equivalent) schema and storage location. - Specify promotion gates and tests for determinism/regeneration. EOF

    cat <<'EOF' > /home/c/Documents/code/ITIR-suite/SensibLaw/src/ingestion/span_role_hypotheses.py from __future__ import annotations import re...
    cat <<'EOF' > /home/c/Documents/code/ITIR-suite/SensibLaw/src/ingestion/span_role_hypotheses.py from __future__ import annotations import re from typing import Iterable, List from src.models.document import Document from src.models.span_role_hypothesis import SpanRoleHypothesis _QUOTED_MEANS = re.compile(r'"(?P<lemma>[^"]{1,80})"\s+means\b', re.IGNORECASE) _SMART_QUOTED_MEANS = re.compile(r"“(?P<lemma>[^”]{1,80})”\s+means\b", re.IGNORECASE) def build_span_role_hypotheses(document: Document) -> List[SpanRoleHypothesis]: """Build deterministic span-only role hypotheses from a document body.""" if not document.body: return [] hypotheses: List[SpanRoleHypothesis] = [] hypotheses.extend(_extract_defined_terms(document.body)) return sorted( hypotheses, key=lambda item: (item.span_start, item.span_end, item.role_hypothesis or ""), ) def _extract_defined_terms(text: str) -> Iterable[SpanRoleHypothesis]: for pattern in (_QUOTED_MEANS, _SMART_QUOTED_MEANS): for match in pattern.finditer(text): term_start = match.start("lemma") term_end = match.end("lemma") yield SpanRoleHypothesis( span_start=term_start, span_end=term_end, span_source="body_char", role_hypothesis="defined_term", extractor="defined_term_regex", evidence="definition", confidence=0.9, metadata={"term_text": match.group("lemma")}, ) __all__ = ["build_span_role_hypotheses"] EOF

    timeout 120s python - <<'PY' import sys from re_gpt.sync_chatgpt import SyncChatGPT from re_gpt.utils import get_session_token, get_default_user_agent, get_default_model from re_gpt.storage import ConversationStorage, extract_ordered_messages phase4_title = "Phase-4 Data Wait" try: token = get_session_token() except Exception as exc: print(f"ERROR: no session token: {exc}", flush=True) sys.exit(1) user_agent = get_default_user_agent() model = get_default_model() with ConversationStorage() as storage, SyncChatGPT( session_token=token, default_model=model, user_agent=user_agent, ) as chatgpt: print("Fetching live catalog...", flush=True) catalog = chatgpt.list_all_conversations() storage.record_conversations(catalog) phase4_matches = [c for c in catalog if (c.get("title") or "").strip().lower() == phase4_title.lower()] if not phase4_matches: print("ERROR: Phase-4 title not found in live catalog.", flush=True) sys.exit(2) def _update_time(conv): return conv.get("update_time") or conv.get("last_updated") or 0 phase4 = max(phase4_matches, key=_update_time) phase4_time = _update_time(phase4) phase4_id = phase4.get("id") print(f"Phase-4 conversation id: {phase4_id}", flush=True) print(f"Phase-4 update_time: {phase4_time}", flush=True) targets = [ c for c in catalog if _update_time(c) and _update_time(c) > phase4_time ] print(f"Live conversations after Phase-4: {len(targets)}", flush=True) for conv in sorted(targets, key=_update_time): conv_id = conv.get("id") title = conv.get("title") or "(no title)" update_time = _update_time(conv) if not conv_id: continue conversation = chatgpt.get_conversation(conv_id, title=title) try: chat = conversation.fetch_chat() except Exception as exc: # noqa: BLE001 print(f"Failed to fetch {conv_id} ({title}): {exc}", flush=True) continue messages = extract_ordered_messages(chat) result = storage.persist_chat(conv_id, chat, messages) print( f"Downloaded {conv_id} | {title} | update_time={update_time} | +{result.new_messages} new", flush=True, ) PY

show a sample of deduped rows with their original source_id and ts for i, (msg_id, row) in enumerate(existing[:10], 1): source_id_row, title, ts, role, text = row snippet = (text or '')[:80].replace('\n', ' ') print(f'{i}. source_id={source_id_row} ts={ts} role={role} title={title} text={snippet!r}') PY
